Actually, in TMHMM, the threshold is based on the HMM scores, not a simple hydrophobicity cutoff. A region with scattered polar residues might still score as a TM if the overall hydrophobicity is high enough. But T, S, and P in the middle of a TM helix would significantly reduce the TM score because they can&#x27;t form a stable helix in the membrane.
